# Graph Report - clever-rutherford  (2026-11-22)

## Corpus Check
- 4053 files · ~6,211,335 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 247 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .ndjson 106, (none) 40, .tex 19)

## Summary
- 36422 nodes · 84301 edges · 1436 communities (1241 shown, 195 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2056 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `297a7e7f`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
